Each story, contextual Jyutping and tones, complete Simplified Chinese gloss, and matching prompt beginning `故事练习：` was manually reviewed. Two content reviewers and a Jyutping reviewer worked read-only; final decisions were made from the current source text and dictionary evidence. Mechanisms include shared-dinner mic timing, fruit-roundabout staging, sauce-leak reversal, order labels as a mock election, lemon-spoon crossover, version testing, rice-water adjustment, the porridge meeting timer, FIFO arrows, mushroom “tea,” coffee checkbox conflict, delivery handoff relay, umbrella-stage improvisation, album/menu confusion, warm-drink overtime, cookie-crumb testing, oil-fried-dough “crispness testing,” ketchup parking lines, and salad “ironing.” No card is family- or money-centered; the office-lunch meeting settings in 03[42,48,49] share a setting but have distinct mechanisms.
